DESIGN.md

show
## Concept
The player is a small dot that can move freely and tap to create a brief temporal field that freezes all nearby obstacles, letting them slip through dense clusters before the field cools down.

## Player
A bright dot, visually a simple neon circle. The player can move in any direction (up, down, left, right) and activate a temporal field with a tap/click. Verbs: move, activate field.

## Core loop
Obstacles continuously spawn and travel across the screen. Each frame the player sees the flow of hazards, decides where to steer, and times the field activation to pause a tight group of obstacles, slipping through the gap. After each use the field enters cooldown, forcing the player to plan the next safe passage. The loop repeats: navigate → anticipate → field → survive → repeat.

## Hazards / Obstacles
- **Sweeping bars** that slide horizontally or vertically, reversing direction after reaching the edge. They act as moving walls that can crush the player.
- **Oscillating spikes** that swing back and forth on a fixed axis, creating rhythmic timing challenges.
- **Rotating blades** that spin around a center point, sweeping a circular area.
- **Static mines** that pulse and expand, becoming lethal when their radius touches the player.
Each hazard moves independently and can overlap, creating dense clusters that require the field to navigate.

## Score
Score increments each time the player successfully passes a hazard cluster without collision, and extra points are awarded for each successful field activation that lets the player escape a dense group. Collecting occasional glowing pickups that appear in safe zones also adds points.

## Win and lose
The player loses immediately upon colliding with any active hazard. The player wins by surviving a final wave of extremely dense obstacles after the field has cooled, signalling mastery of timing and navigation. When the game ends, a “Game Over” or “You Win!” overlay appears with the final score and a prompt to restart.

## Feel
Fast‑paced and tense, with neon‑bright visual cues against a dark background. The field flash gives a brief moment of stillness, then the world rushes back, creating a rhythm of frantic movement punctuated by short pauses. Audio consists of crisp, synth stabs for taps and subtle hums for hazards.

## Why it's fun
The thrill of timing a single, powerful pause to slip through an impossible‑looking wall makes the player feel like a time‑bending hero at every successful escape.

TASKS.md

show
1. HTML scaffold + meta.json — create index.html with required header, canvas, restart button and empty script block; create meta.json matching header information.
2. Game state objects — define player, hazards, score, phase, and input structures as plain object literals at the top of the script.
3. Main loop implementation — add startLoop() with cancelAnimationFrame handling, a requestAnimationFrame loop wrapped in try/catch that calls update(dt) and render().
4. Input handling — set up a keys Set with keydown/keyup listeners; add R-key and restart button hooks that call a shared reset() function.
5. Player movement — implement dt‑scaled physics for acceleration, max speed, friction, and canvas bounds; ensure movement updates visible player coordinates.
6. Hazard system — create data structures for obstacles, spawn rules respecting SAFE_RADIUS at t=0, and dt‑scaled motion for each hazard type.
7. Collision detection — check player vs hazards each frame; on collision set state to "gameover" and stop active gameplay.
8. Scoring — increase score on discrete events (e.g., passing a hazard or successful field activation) and render the score at the top‑left of the canvas.
9. Rendering — clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player (high‑contrast neon), and HUD elements each frame.
10. Game‑over overlay — dim the background and display "Game Over" with final score and restart instructions.
11. Observable contract — populate window.__game each frame with state, score, player position/visibility, and a concise objective sentence.
12. Temporal field ability — implement tap/click activation that creates a short‑lived field freezing nearby obstacles; include cooldown logic.
13. Audio integration — lazy‑initialize AudioContext in a beep() helper and trigger sounds for hit, score, and field activation, wrapped in try/catch.
14. Final polish & verification — fine‑tune constants (speeds, durations, cooldowns), ensure no console errors, and confirm meta.json matches the header text.

show source (8,378 chars)
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>Pulse Freeze</title>
  <style></style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Pulse Freeze</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Tap to generate a short‑lived temporal field around the dot that pauses all on‑screen obstacles for a brief moment, letting you slip through dense clusters; the field then cools down before it can be used again.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Arrow keys (or WASD) to move</li>
      <li>Tap or click to activate field</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
// === CONFIG =============================================================
// Config constants
const FIELD_RADIUS = 80; // radius of temporal field, px
const FIELD_DURATION = 0.5; // seconds
const FIELD_COOLDOWN = 3.0; // seconds

// Player physics constants
const PLAYER_ACCEL = 300; // px/sec^2
const PLAYER_MAX_SPEED = 400; // px/sec
const PLAYER_FRICTION = 0.85; // per second friction factor

// Hazard constants
const HAZARD_SPEED = 120; // px/sec
const HAZARD_SIZE = 20; // size of square hazard, px

// === STATE ==============================================================
const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

let player = { x: canvas.width / 2, y: canvas.height / 2, vx: 0, vy: 0, radius: 8 };
let hazards = []; // array of hazard objects
let score = 0;
let highScore = 0;
let phase = "menu"; // "menu" | "playing" | "gameover"
let keys = new Set();
let lastTime = performance.now();
let loopId = null;
let audioCtx = null;
// === LOOP ==============================================================
function startLoop() {
  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  function tick(now) {
    try {
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;
      update(dt);
      render();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("loop error", err);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}
// === RESET ==============================================================
function reset() {
  player = { x: canvas.width / 2, y: canvas.height / 2, vx: 0, vy: 0, radius: 8 };
  hazards = [];
  score = 0;
  phase = "playing";
  keys.clear();
  lastTime = performance.now();
  fieldCooldown = 0;
  fieldTimer = 0;
  // spawn initial hazards
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) spawnHazard();
  startLoop();
}

function spawnHazard() {
  const SAFE_RADIUS = 120;
  let x, y;
  do {
    x = Math.random() * canvas.width;
    y = Math.random() * canvas.height;
  } while (Math.hypot(x - player.x, y - player.y) < SAFE_RADIUS);
  const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
  const speed = HAZARD_SPEED;
  hazards.push({
    x,
    y,
    vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,
    vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
    size: HAZARD_SIZE,
  });
}

// === UPDATE ==============================================================
let fieldTimer = 0;
let fieldCooldown = 0;
function update(dt) {
  if (phase !== "playing") return;
  // Player input
  let ax = 0, ay = 0;
  if (keys.has("ArrowLeft") || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A")) ax -= 1;
  if (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D")) ax += 1;
  if (keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W")) ay -= 1;
  if (keys.has("ArrowDown") || keys.has("s") || keys.has("S")) ay += 1;
  if (ax !== 0 || ay !== 0) {
    const len = Math.hypot(ax, ay);
    ax = (ax / len) * PLAYER_ACCEL;
    ay = (ay / len) * PLAYER_ACCEL;
  }
  player.vx += ax * dt;
  player.vy += ay * dt;
  // friction
  player.vx *= Math.pow(PLAYER_FRICTION, dt);
  player.vy *= Math.pow(PLAYER_FRICTION, dt);
  // clamp speed
  const sp = Math.hypot(player.vx, player.vy);
  if (sp > PLAYER_MAX_SPEED) {
    const s = PLAYER_MAX_SPEED / sp;
    player.vx *= s;
    player.vy *= s;
  }
  // move player with wraparound
  player.x = (player.x + player.vx * dt + canvas.width) % canvas.width;
  player.y = (player.y + player.vy * dt + canvas.height) % canvas.height;

  // field timers
  if (fieldCooldown > 0) fieldCooldown -= dt;
  if (fieldTimer > 0) {
    fieldTimer -= dt;
    if (fieldTimer <= 0) {
      // field ended
    }
  }

  // hazards movement (paused while field active)
  if (fieldTimer <= 0) {
    for (const h of hazards) {
      h.x = (h.x + h.vx * dt + canvas.width) % canvas.width;
      h.y = (h.y + h.vy * dt + canvas.height) % canvas.height;
    }
  }

  // collision detection when field not active
  if (fieldTimer <= 0) {
    for (const h of hazards) {
      const dx = h.x - player.x;
      const dy = h.y - player.y;
      const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      if (dist < h.size / 2 + player.radius) {
        phase = "gameover";
        if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
        beep(110, 0.3);
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  // simple scoring: increment each frame
  score = Math.floor((performance.now() - lastTime) / 1000);
}

// === RENDER ==============================================================
function render() {
  // background
  ctx.fillStyle = "#05050c";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  // field effect
  if (fieldTimer > 0) {
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.2)";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, FIELD_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }
  // hazards
  ctx.fillStyle = "#ff5200";
  for (const h of hazards) {
    ctx.fillRect(h.x - h.size/2, h.y - h.size/2, h.size, h.size);
  }
  // player
  ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, player.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();

  // HUD
  ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
  ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
  ctx.textAlign = "left";
  ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
  if (phase === "gameover") {
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.6)";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ff52ae";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.fillText("Game Over", canvas.width/2, canvas.height/2 - 20);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, canvas.width/2, canvas.height/2 + 14);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", canvas.width/2, canvas.height/2 + 42);
  }
}

// === INPUT ==============================================================
document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
  if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") {
    reset();
    return;
  }
  keys.add(ev.key);
  if (ev.key === " " || ev.key === "Space") ev.preventDefault();
});

document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);
canvas.addEventListener("mousedown", (ev) => {
  if (fieldCooldown <= 0 && phase === "playing") {
    fieldTimer = FIELD_DURATION;
    fieldCooldown = FIELD_COOLDOWN;
    beep(440, 0.1);
  }
});

// === AUDIO (lazy) ======================================================
function beep(freq, durSec) {
  try {
    if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
    const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
    osc.type = "triangle";
    osc.frequency.value = freq;
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, audioCtx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
  } catch {}
}

// === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
function publishState() {
  window.__game = {
    state: phase,
    score,
    player: {
      x: player.x,
      y: player.y,
      visible: player.x >= 0 && player.x < canvas.width && player.y >= 0 && player.y < canvas.height,
    },
    objective: "Tap to generate a short‑lived temporal field that pauses obstacles, letting you slip through dense clusters.",
  };
}

// === BOOT ===============================================================
reset();
</script>
</body>
</html>

## Concept

```
// === RESET ==============================================================
function reset() {
  player = { x: canvas.width / 2, y: canvas.height / 2, vx: 0, vy: 0, radius: 8 };
  hazards = [];
  score = 0;
  phase = "playing";
  keys.clear();
  lastTime = performance.now();
  fieldCooldown = 0;
  fieldTimer = 0;
  // spawn initial hazards
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) spawnHazard();
  startLoop();
}

// Helper to spawn hazard away from player safe radius
function spawnHazard() {
  const SAFE_RADIUS = 120;
  let x, y;
  do {
    x = Math.random() * canvas.width;
    y = Math.random() * canvas.height;
  } while (Math.hypot(x - player.x, y - player.y) < SAFE_RADIUS);
  const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
  const speed = HAZARD_SPEED;
  hazards.push({
    x,
    y,
    vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,
    vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
    size: HAZARD_SIZE,
  });
}

// === UPDATE ==============================================================
let fieldTimer = 0;
let fieldCooldown = 0;
function update(dt) {
  if (phase !== "playing") return;
  // player input
  let ax = 0, ay = 0;
  if (keys.has("ArrowLeft") || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A")) ax -= 1;
  if (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D")) ax += 1;
  if (keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W")) ay -= 1;
  if (keys.has("ArrowDown") || keys.has("s") || keys.has("S")) ay += 1;
  if (ax !== 0 || ay !== 0) {
    const len = Math.hypot(ax, ay);
    ax = (ax / len) * PLAYER_ACCEL;
    ay = (ay / len) * PLAYER_ACCEL;
  }
  player.vx += ax * dt;
  player.vy += ay * dt;
  // friction
  player.vx *= Math.pow(PLAYER_FRICTION, dt);
  player.vy *= Math.pow(PLAYER_FRICTION, dt);
  // clamp speed
  const sp = Math.hypot(player.vx, player.vy);
  if (sp > PLAYER_MAX_SPEED) {
    const s = PLAYER_MAX_SPEED / sp;
    player.vx *= s;
    player.vy *= s;
  }
  // move
  player.x = (player.x + player.vx * dt + canvas.width) % canvas.width;
  player.y = (player.y + player.vy * dt + canvas.height) % canvas.height;

  // field timers
  if (fieldCooldown > 0) fieldCooldown -= dt;
  if (fieldTimer > 0) {
    fieldTimer -= dt;
    if (fieldTimer <= 0) {
      // field ended
      // maybe beep
    }
  }

  // hazards movement (paused if field active)
  if (fieldTimer <= 0) {
    for (const h of hazards) {
      h.x = (h.x + h.vx * dt + canvas.width) % canvas.width;
      h.y = (h.y + h.vy * dt + canvas.height) % canvas.height;
    }
  }

  // collision detection when field not active
  if (fieldTimer <= 0) {
    for (const h of hazards) {
      const dx = h.x - player.x;
      const dy = h.y - player.y;
      const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      if (dist < h.size / 2 + player.radius) {
        phase = "gameover";
        if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
        beep(110, 0.3);
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  // simple scoring: increase each frame
  score = Math.floor((performance.now() - lastTime) / 1000);
}

// === RENDER ==============================================================
function render() {
  // background
  ctx.fillStyle = "#05050c";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  // field effect
  if (fieldTimer > 0) {
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2)";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, FIELD_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }
  // hazards
  ctx.fillStyle = "#ff5200";
  for (const h of hazards) {
    ctx.fillRect(h.x - h.size/2, h.y - h.size/2, h.size, h.size);
  }
  // player
  ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, player.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();

  // HUD
  ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
  ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
  ctx.textAlign = "left";
  ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
  if (phase === "gameover") {
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.6)";
    ctx.fillRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ff52ae";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.fillText("Game Over", canvas.width/2, canvas.height/2 - 20);
    ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, canvas.width/2, canvas.height/2 + 14);
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", canvas.width/2, canvas.height/2 + 42);
  }
}

// === INPUT ==============================================================
document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
  if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") {
    reset();
    return;
  }
  keys.add(ev.key);
  if (ev.key === " " || ev.key === "Space") ev.preventDefault();
});
document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => {
  keys.delete(ev.key);
});
restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);
canvas.addEventListener("mousedown", (ev) => {
  if (fieldCooldown <= 0 && phase === "playing") {
    fieldTimer = FIELD_DURATION;
    fieldCooldown = FIELD_COOLDOWN;
    beep(440, 0.1);
  }
});

// === AUDIO (lazy) ======================================================
function beep(freq, durSec) {
  try {
    if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
    const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
    osc.type = "triangle";
    osc.frequency.value = freq;
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, audioCtx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
  } catch {}
}

// === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ================================================
function publishState() {
  window.__game = {
    state: phase,
    score,
    player: {
      x: player.x,
      y: player.y,
      visible: player.x >= 0 && player.x < canvas.width && player.y >= 0 && player.y < canvas.height,
    },
    objective: "Tap to generate a short‑lived temporal field that pauses obstacles, letting you slip through dense clusters.",
  };
}

// === BOOT ==============================================================
reset();
```

Validation errors:
- playtest: window.__game is not exposed. Required shape: { state: "menu"|"playing"|"gameover", score: number, player: { x: number, y: number, visible: boolean }, objective: string }. Set this on the very first frame, before any input.

This is fix iteration 5 of 6. After your edits, the page will be reloaded and re-validated. If errors persist after 6 iterations, this game is abandoned.

Common interpretations:
- "canvas appears blank" → the game is loading but `render()` isn't drawing anything. Check that `startLoop()` actually got called at boot, that `render()` runs every frame, and that you're issuing `ctx.fill*` / `ctx.stroke*` calls per entity each frame (not just clearing the canvas).
- "controls list mismatch" → the `<ul class="game-controls">` in `index.html` and the `controls` array in `meta.json` disagree. Pick one source of truth, edit the other to match exactly.
- "playtest: player.visible was false for X% of frames" → the camera/position math is letting the player drift outside `[0, canvas.width) × [0, canvas.height)`. Either clamp the player or update `player.visible` to reflect the truth (don't just hardcode `true`).
- "playtest: state took invalid value" → `window.__game.state` must be exactly `"menu"`, `"playing"`, or `"gameover"`. No other strings.
- "Required file missing: meta.json" → you didn't write the file. Use your write tool now.
- "pageerror" / "console.error" → an exception is being thrown at runtime. Read the message, locate the line, fix the bug.

**Use your write/edit tool to apply the fixes.** Do NOT respond with code in chat — the files on disk are what gets validated.

prompt (15,984 chars)
## Reference example: "asteroids"

The following is a complete, validator-passing game. **Use it as a structural template** — your output must follow the same section ordering, the same patterns for the loop / restart / observable contract, and the same overall code style. Do NOT reproduce this game; you are building a different game described in the prompt below. The example exists only to anchor structure.

Notice in particular:
- The `// === SECTION ===` divider banners and their fixed order: CONFIG → STATE → RESET → LOOP/UPDATE → RENDER → INPUT → AUDIO → OBSERVABLE CONTRACT → BOOT.
- Plain object literals for game state (no `class`).
- A `keys` Set polled each frame instead of mutating game state from key handlers.
- `if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` at the top of `startLoop()`.
- The animation loop body wrapped in `try/catch`.
- `publishState()` called once per frame at the end of the tick.
- `window.__game` populated honestly (player.visible reflects actual viewport position).
- AudioContext initialized lazily inside `beep()`, wrapped in try/catch.

### Example `meta.json`
```json
{
  "title": "Neon Asteroids",
  "description": "Rotate, thrust, and shoot. Big rocks split into small ones. Get hit and you're gone.",
  "controls": [
    "Left/Right (or A/D) to rotate",
    "Up (or W) to thrust",
    "Space to fire",
    "Press R or click Restart"
  ],
  "tags": ["arcade", "shooter", "physics"]
}
```

### Example `index.html`
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>Neon Asteroids</title>
  <style>
    body { margin: 0; background: #0a0a0f; color: #e6e6f0; font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif; }
    .game-header { padding: 12px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid #1d1d2a; }
    .game-header h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 18px; color: #7df9ff; }
    .game-header .game-description { margin: 4px 0; color: #a5a5b8; }
    .game-header .game-controls { margin: 4px 0; padding-left: 18px; color: #c8c8d8; }
    main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; padding: 16px; }
    canvas { background: #05050c; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; }
    .game-restart { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; background: #1c2030; color: #7df9ff; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; cursor: pointer; }
    .game-restart:hover { background: #232842; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Neon Asteroids</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Rotate, thrust, and shoot. Big rocks split into small ones. Get hit and you're gone.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Left/Right (or A/D) to rotate</li>
      <li>Up (or W) to thrust</li>
      <li>Space to fire</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
  // === CONFIG =============================================================
  const W = 800, H = 600;
  const SHIP_RADIUS = 12;
  const SHIP_THRUST = 280;       // px/sec^2
  const SHIP_FRICTION = 0.6;     // velocity decay per second (1 = none)
  const SHIP_TURN = Math.PI * 1.8; // rad/sec
  const BULLET_SPEED = 520;      // px/sec
  const BULLET_LIFE = 1.0;       // sec
  const FIRE_COOLDOWN = 0.20;    // sec between shots
  const ASTEROID_BIG_R = 38;
  const ASTEROID_MID_R = 22;
  const ASTEROID_LIL_R = 12;
  const ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE = [40, 110];
  const SAFE_RADIUS_AT_SPAWN = 160; // no asteroid spawns inside this disk

  const SCORE_BIG = 20, SCORE_MID = 50, SCORE_LIL = 100;

  // === STATE ==============================================================
  const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

  let ship;        // { x, y, vx, vy, angle, alive }
  let bullets;     // [{ x, y, vx, vy, life }]
  let asteroids;   // [{ x, y, vx, vy, r, shape: number[] }]
  let score;
  let highScore = 0;
  let phase;       // "playing" | "gameover"
  let fireCooldown;
  let keys;
  let lastTime;
  let loopId = null;
  let audioCtx = null;

  function reset() {
    ship = { x: W / 2, y: H / 2, vx: 0, vy: 0, angle: -Math.PI / 2, alive: true };
    bullets = [];
    asteroids = [];
    spawnWave(5);
    score = 0;
    phase = "playing";
    fireCooldown = 0;
    keys = new Set();
    lastTime = performance.now();
    publishState();
    startLoop();
  }

  function startLoop() {
    if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
    function tick(now) {
      try {
        const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
        lastTime = now;
        update(dt);
        render();
        publishState();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("loop error", err);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  function spawnWave(n) {
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) spawnAsteroid(ASTEROID_BIG_R);
  }

  function spawnAsteroid(r) {
    let x, y, tries = 0;
    do {
      x = Math.random() * W;
      y = Math.random() * H;
      tries++;
    } while (Math.hypot(x - ship.x, y - ship.y) < SAFE_RADIUS_AT_SPAWN && tries < 30);
    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    const speed = ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0] + Math.random() * (ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[1] - ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0]);
    asteroids.push({
      x, y,
      vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,
      vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
      r,
      shape: makeAsteroidShape(),
    });
  }

  function makeAsteroidShape() {
    // Per-vertex radius multipliers, 8-12 vertices for a knobbly silhouette.
    const n = 8 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 5);
    const out = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) out.push(0.75 + Math.random() * 0.45);
    return out;
  }

  // === UPDATE =============================================================
  function update(dt) {
    if (phase !== "playing") return;

    // Ship.
    if (ship.alive) {
      const turnIn = (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D") ? 1 : 0)
                   - (keys.has("ArrowLeft")  || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A") ? 1 : 0);
      ship.angle += turnIn * SHIP_TURN * dt;

      const thrusting = keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W");
      if (thrusting) {
        ship.vx += Math.cos(ship.angle) * SHIP_THRUST * dt;
        ship.vy += Math.sin(ship.angle) * SHIP_THRUST * dt;
      }
      // Mild friction so the ship doesn't drift forever.
      const decay = Math.pow(SHIP_FRICTION, dt);
      ship.vx *= decay;
      ship.vy *= decay;
      ship.x = wrap(ship.x + ship.vx * dt, W);
      ship.y = wrap(ship.y + ship.vy * dt, H);

      fireCooldown -= dt;
      if ((keys.has(" ") || keys.has("Space")) && fireCooldown <= 0) {
        fire();
        fireCooldown = FIRE_COOLDOWN;
      }
    }

    // Bullets.
    for (const b of bullets) {
      b.life -= dt;
      b.x = wrap(b.x + b.vx * dt, W);
      b.y = wrap(b.y + b.vy * dt, H);
    }
    bullets = bullets.filter((b) => b.life > 0);

    // Asteroids.
    for (const a of asteroids) {
      a.x = wrap(a.x + a.vx * dt, W);
      a.y = wrap(a.y + a.vy * dt, H);
    }

    // Bullet-asteroid collisions.
    for (let i = asteroids.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const a = asteroids[i];
      let hit = false;
      for (let j = bullets.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
        const b = bullets[j];
        if (Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y) < a.r) {
          bullets.splice(j, 1);
          hit = true;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (hit) splitAsteroid(i);
    }

    // Ship-asteroid collisions.
    if (ship.alive) {
      for (const a of asteroids) {
        if (Math.hypot(a.x - ship.x, a.y - ship.y) < a.r + SHIP_RADIUS * 0.6) {
          ship.alive = false;
          phase = "gameover";
          if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
          beep(110, 0.3);
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    // Wave clear -> spawn next.
    if (asteroids.length === 0 && phase === "playing") {
      spawnWave(5);
    }
  }

  function fire() {
    bullets.push({
      x: ship.x + Math.cos(ship.angle) * SHIP_RADIUS,
      y: ship.y + Math.sin(ship.angle) * SHIP_RADIUS,
      vx: ship.vx + Math.cos(ship.angle) * BULLET_SPEED,
      vy: ship.vy + Math.sin(ship.angle) * BULLET_SPEED,
      life: BULLET_LIFE,
    });
    beep(820, 0.04);
  }

  function splitAsteroid(idx) {
    const a = asteroids[idx];
    asteroids.splice(idx, 1);
    if (a.r === ASTEROID_BIG_R) {
      score += SCORE_BIG;
      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) addChild(a, ASTEROID_MID_R);
      beep(440, 0.06);
    } else if (a.r === ASTEROID_MID_R) {
      score += SCORE_MID;
      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) addChild(a, ASTEROID_LIL_R);
      beep(560, 0.05);
    } else {
      score += SCORE_LIL;
      beep(700, 0.05);
    }
  }

  function addChild(parent, r) {
    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    const speed = ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0] + Math.random() * (ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[1] - ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0]);
    asteroids.push({
      x: parent.x, y: parent.y,
      vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,
      vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
      r,
      shape: makeAsteroidShape(),
    });
  }

  // === RENDER =============================================================
  function render() {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#05050c";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

    // Asteroids.
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#a5a5b8";
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    for (const a of asteroids) drawAsteroid(a);

    // Bullets.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ffd23f";
    for (const b of bullets) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, 2.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }

    // Ship.
    if (ship.alive) drawShip();

    // HUD.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
    ctx.textAlign = "right";
    ctx.fillText(`Best ${highScore}`, W - 12, 22);

    if (phase === "gameover") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(5,5,12,0.65)";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ff52ae";
      ctx.fillText("Game Over", W / 2, H / 2 - 20);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, W / 2, H / 2 + 14);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", W / 2, H / 2 + 42);
    }
  }

  function drawShip() {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(ship.x, ship.y);
    ctx.rotate(ship.angle);
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#7df9ff";
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(SHIP_RADIUS, 0);
    ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.9, SHIP_RADIUS * 0.7);
    ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.5, 0);
    ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.9, -SHIP_RADIUS * 0.7);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.stroke();
    if (keys && (keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W"))) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = "#ffd23f";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.5, 0);
      ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS - 6 - Math.random() * 4, 0);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  function drawAsteroid(a) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    for (let i = 0; i < a.shape.length; i++) {
      const t = (i / a.shape.length) * Math.PI * 2;
      const r = a.r * a.shape[i];
      const x = a.x + Math.cos(t) * r;
      const y = a.y + Math.sin(t) * r;
      if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y); else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
    }
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  // === INPUT ==============================================================
  document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
    if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
    keys.add(ev.key);
    if (ev.key === " ") ev.preventDefault();
  });
  document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
  restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

  // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
  function beep(freq, durSec) {
    try {
      if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
      osc.type = "triangle";
      osc.frequency.value = freq;
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
      osc.start(); osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    } catch {}
  }

  // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
  function publishState() {
    window.__game = {
      state: phase,                                  // "playing" | "gameover"
      score,
      player: {
        x: ship.x,
        y: ship.y,
        // wrap() keeps coords in [0, W) and [0, H), so visible is always true
        // — but assert it explicitly so the contract is honest.
        visible: ship.x >= 0 && ship.x < W && ship.y >= 0 && ship.y < H,
      },
      objective: "Shoot the asteroids before they hit your ship. Big rocks split into smaller ones.",
    };
  }

  // === HELPERS ============================================================
  function wrap(v, max) {
    if (v < 0) return v + max;
    if (v >= max) return v - max;
    return v;
  }

  // === BOOT ===============================================================
  reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

    function tick(now) {

function startLoop() {

Implementation:

```js
// Player input
let ax = 0, ay = 0;
if (keys.has("ArrowLeft") || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A")) ax -= 1;
if (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D")) ax += 1;
if (keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W")) ay -= 1;
if (keys.has("ArrowDown") || keys.has("s") || keys.has("S")) ay += 1;

// Normalize diagonal speed
if (ax !== 0 || ay !== 0) {
  const len = Math.hypot(ax, ay);
  ax = (ax / len) * PLAYER_ACCEL;
  ay = (ay / len) * PLAYER_ACCEL;
}

// Apply acceleration
player.vx += ax * dt;
player.vy += ay * dt;

// Apply friction
player.vx *= Math.pow(PLAYER_FRICTION, dt);
player.vy *= Math.pow(PLAYER_FRICTION, dt);

// Clamp speed
const speed = Math.hypot(player.vx, player.vy);
if (speed > PLAYER_MAX_SPEED) {
  const scale = PLAYER_MAX_SPEED / speed;
  player.vx *= scale;
  player.vy *= scale;
}

// Update position
player.x = (player.x + player.vx * dt + canvas.width) % canvas.width;
player.y = (player.y + player.vy * dt + canvas.height) % canvas.height;
```

Let's construct newString.

```
<script>
// === CONFIG =============================================================
// Player physics constants
const PLAYER_ACCEL = 300; // px/sec^2
const PLAYER_MAX_SPEED = 400; // px/sec
const PLAYER_FRICTION = 0.85; // per second friction factor

// === STATE ==============================================================
const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

let player = { x: canvas.width / 2, y: canvas.height / 2, vx: 0, vy: 0, radius: 8 };
let hazards = []; // array of hazard objects
let score = 0;
let highScore = 0;
let phase = "menu"; // "menu" | "playing" | "gameover"
let keys = new Set();
let lastTime = performance.now();
let loopId = null;
let audioCtx = null;

// === RESET ==============================================================
function reset() {
  player = { x: canvas.width / 2, y: canvas.height / 2, vx: 0, vy: 0, radius: 8 };
  hazards = [];
  score = 0;
  phase = "playing";
  keys.clear();
  lastTime = performance.now();
  startLoop();
}

// === LOOP ==============================================================
function startLoop() {
  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  function tick(now) {
    try {
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;
      update(dt);
      render();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("loop error", err);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}

// === UPDATE =============================================================
function update(dt) {
  if (phase !== "playing") return;

  // Player input handling
  let ax = 0, ay = 0;
  if (keys.has("ArrowLeft") || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A")) ax -= 1;
  if (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D")) ax += 1;
  if (keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W")) ay -= 1;
  if (keys.has("ArrowDown") || keys.has("s") || keys.has("S")) ay += 1;

  // Normalize diagonal acceleration
  if (ax !== 0 || ay !== 0) {
    const len = Math.hypot(ax, ay);
    ax = (ax / len) * PLAYER_ACCEL;
    ay = (ay / len) * PLAYER_ACCEL;
  }

  // Apply acceleration
  player.vx += ax * dt;
  player.vy += ay * dt;

  // Apply friction
  player.vx *= Math.pow(PLAYER_FRICTION, dt);
  player.vy *= Math.pow(PLAYER_FRICTION, dt);

  // Clamp speed
  const speed = Math.hypot(player.vx, player.vy);
  if (speed > PLAYER_MAX_SPEED) {
    const scale = PLAYER_MAX_SPEED / speed;
    player.vx *= scale;
    player.vy *= scale;
  }

  // Update position with wraparound
  player.x = (player.x + player.vx * dt + canvas.width) % canvas.width;
  player.y = (player.y + player.vy * dt + canvas.height) % canvas.height;
}

// === RENDER =============================================================
function render() {
  // Clear canvas
  ctx.fillStyle = "#05050c";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

  // Draw player
  ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, player.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();

  // Draw score / UI placeholder
  ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
  ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
  ctx.textAlign = "left";
  ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
}

// === INPUT ==============================================================
document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
  if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") {
    reset();
    return;
  }
  keys.add(ev.key);
  if (ev.key === " ") ev.preventDefault();
});
document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => {
  keys.delete(ev.key);
});
restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

// === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
function beep(freq, durSec) {
  try {
    if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
    const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
    osc.type = "triangle";
    osc.frequency.value = freq;
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, audioCtx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
  } catch {}
}

// === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ================================================
function publishState() {
  window.__game = {
    state: phase,
    score,
    player: {
      x: player.x,
      y: player.y,
      visible: true // player always visible due to wraparound
    },
    objective: "Tap to generate a short‑lived temporal field that pauses obstacles, letting you slip through dense clusters."
  };
}

// === BOOT ===============================================================
reset();
</script>
```

We also need to include call to publishState maybe after each frame. Not yet. But for now page loads cleanly; not required now for tasks, but for later tasks they will need observable contract. However we can add publishState call within startLoop after render. But we could also add later. But maybe include now to be safe. Task 3 didn't mention publishing state; that's later. But we don't need it now; but adding will not break.

But adding publishState after each tick: In startLoop tick after render, we can call publishState(). This is safe. Let's add.

Add after render call:

```

publishState();

// === FIELD ==============================================================
function activateField() {
  if (fieldCooldown <= 0 && !fieldActive) {
    fieldActive = true;
    fieldTimer = FIELD_DURATION;
    fieldCooldown = FIELD_COOLDOWN;
    // Optionally play sound: beep(...);
  }
}

// Update field timers (to be called from update)
function updateField(dt) {
  if (fieldTimer > 0) {
    fieldTimer -= dt;
    if (fieldTimer <= 0) {
      fieldActive = false;
      fieldTimer = 0;
    }
  }
  if (fieldCooldown > 0) {
    fieldCooldown -= dt;
    if (fieldCooldown < 0) fieldCooldown = 0;
  }
}

But note we must ensure update(dt) function calls updateField(dt). However update not defined yet; later tasks may call update update. We can either define update function now with some placeholder that includes player movement? But that will be added later maybe.

function render() {
  // Clear canvas
  ctx.fillStyle = "#05050c";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

  // Draw player
  ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, player.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();

  // Draw field if active
  if (fieldActive) {
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.2)";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, FIELD_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }
}

Your job in this stage is to break the implementation into a numbered list of small, ordered tasks. The next stage will work through this list one task at a time, in a single LLM session, reloading the page after each task and capturing console errors for you to fix.

Title: Pulse Freeze
Concept: Tap to generate a short-lived temporal field around the dot that pauses all on-screen obstacles for a brief moment, letting you slip through dense clusters; the field then cools down before it can be used again.

**You must produce one output file: `TASKS.md` in the working directory.** Use your write/edit tool to create this file on disk. After your turn ends, the validator runs `readFile("TASKS.md")` — if the file does not exist on disk, validation fails and your work is wasted. **Do NOT reply with the task list as inline chat text.** The list must be written to the file.

**Tool calls MUST be emitted as actual function/tool invocations — NOT described or simulated inside your reasoning/analysis text.** Writing tool-shaped JSON like `{"filePath":"TASKS.md","content":"..."}` *inside your chain-of-thought* does NOT invoke any tool. It is just text in the analysis channel that nothing reads or executes. The same goes for `{"pattern":"**/*"}` etc. — those only do something when emitted as a real `tool_use` event from the action channel, never when written into your thinking. Several prior runs have failed by hallucinating tool calls inside reasoning; do not repeat that pattern. If at any point you "describe" a tool call in your thinking instead of invoking it, you must immediately invoke the real tool afterward. The format MUST be a numbered list, one task per line, each line starting with `<number>.`:

```
1. <Task title> — <one-sentence description of what to do>
2. <Task title> — <one-sentence description>
...
```

The next stage parses tasks by matching `^\d+\.` at the start of each line. Don't use bullet sub-lists, don't number sub-tasks (1.1, 1.2). One number, one task, one line.

### What makes a good task

- **Small enough** to complete in a single coding turn (~50–150 lines of edits).
- **Testable**: after this task, what does the page do that it didn't before?
- **Leaves the file runnable**: at the end of the task, no syntax errors, no uncaught exceptions on load. Even if the game isn't playable yet, it must load.
- **Specific**: don't say "implement player". Say "implement player movement: arrow keys set keys.left/right; in update(), apply acceleration in the pressed direction with cap at MAX_SPEED, friction otherwise; clamp x to canvas bounds."
- **Concrete numbers**: this is where pixel sizes, ms intervals, and px/sec speeds live. Pick them. The implement stage will use them verbatim.

### Required coverage (in roughly this order)

Cover at minimum, ordered as below. Add tasks if the design demands more (powerups, level progression, screen wrap, etc):

1. **HTML scaffold + meta.json**: header (h1, description, controls list), canvas, restart button per CONVENTIONS.md. Stub `<script>` block (empty). meta.json matching header text.
2. **Game state objects**: player, hazards, score, phase using plain object literals at the top of the script. Set initial values.
3. **requestAnimationFrame loop**: startLoop() with `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` discipline at the top. Body wrapped in try/catch. Calls update(dt) and render(). Don't draw anything yet besides clearing the canvas.
4. **Input**: a `keys` Set populated by document keydown/keyup. R-key restart hook. Restart button click hook. Both call the same reset() function.
5. **Player movement**: dt-based physics tied to the key inputs from task 4. Verify each control listed in meta.json actually changes a value.
6. **Hazards**: data structure, spawn rules with a SAFE_RADIUS so nothing spawns on top of the player at t=0, motion (dt-scaled).
7. **Collision detection**: player vs hazards. On hit, transition phase to "gameover" and stop the active gameplay.
8. **Score**: discrete events that increase score, drawn on canvas at top-left.
9. **Render**: clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player, HUD. Player must be the highest-contrast object on screen.
10. **Game-over overlay**: dim background, "Game Over", final score, "Press R or click Restart".
11. **Observable contract**: populate `window.__game = { state, score, player:{x,y,visible}, objective }` every frame. `state` is "playing" | "gameover". `objective` is a 10–200 char single-sentence description.
12. **Audio**: lazy-init AudioContext inside a beep() helper, wrapped in try/catch. Hook into hit / score / death events.

You may merge two trivial tasks if they truly belong together (e.g. tasks 1+2 if state is just three lines). You may split a complex task in two. Aim for 10–15 tasks total.

Never make the player wait.\n92: 2. **Clear feedback** — the player always knows when something happened. Use color flashes, particles, brief screen shake, score popups, or sound on every meaningful event (hit, score, miss, death).\n93: 3. **Win/lose state** — the game has a clear end condition. On lose, draw \"Game Over\" + final score + restart instructions on the canvas. On win, celebrate (color burst, \"You Win!\" overlay).\n94: 4. **Restart without page reload** — both pressing R AND clicking the `<button id=\"restart\">` reset the game cleanly via the same code path. Mention both in `controls` (e.g. \"Press R or click Restart\"). On restart you MUST `cancelAnimationFrame` any pending loop before starting a new one (otherwise loops stack on each restart and the game eventually slows to a crawl).\n95: 5. **Progressive difficulty** — start gentle so the player can learn, then ramp up. Don't dump them into chaos at t=0.\n96: 6. **No dead time** — something interesting on screen at all times. Idle background animation (stars, gradient pulse, particles) is fine.\n97: 7. **Persistent score / progress UI** — drawn on the canvas at the top corner, always visible.\n98: 8. **Audio (optional but encouraged)** — generate tones with `OscillatorNode` (no external assets). **Do not auto-start audio on page load** — browsers block this and it will throw. Initialize the `AudioContext` lazily on the first keypress or click. Wrap audio code in try/catch.\n99: \n100: ## Playable Contract — non-negotiable\n101: \n102: These are not aspirations. A game that violates any of these is broken even if it runs without errors. After your implementation finishes, the validator loads the page in a real browser and runs an 8-second playtest with synthetic input — failures here are reported back to you and must be fixed before the game ships.\n103: \n104: ### 1. The player can lose\n105: \n106: There must be a collision check (or other end condition) that fires `gameOver` and stops the active gameplay loop. \"Game runs forever, no death\" is broken. Examples that have failed in the past: collision check compared a value against itself; \"spike\" collision only clamped position instead of killing the player; obstacles never reached the player.\n107: \n108: ### 2. The player can score\n109: \n110: The score must increase on a **discrete event** (a survived obstacle, a collected pickup, an enemy destroyed, a beat hit). \"Score = elapsed seconds\" is allowed but weak. When spawning hazards, **reject any spawn that is within `SAFE_RADIUS` of the player AND would reach them within 3 seconds at current hazard speed**. Pre-spawning hazards on top of the player at t=0 has caused frame-1 deaths in past games. Don't do it.\n115: \n116: ### 4. The control actually changes the outcome\n117: \n118: Whatever the player presses must, in the next frame, change a value that affects collision or score. If a control is purely cosmetic (e.g. \"stretch horizontally\" but the gaps are vertical), the game is fundamentally broken even if it runs. Per-frame constants (`x += 5` every frame) make games run 2.4× faster on a 144Hz display.\n123: \n124: ```js\n125: let lastTime = performance.now();\n126: function loop(now) {\n127:   const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1); // seconds, clamped\n128:   lastTime = now;\n129:   // physics scaled by dt:\n130:   player.x += player.vx * dt;\n131:   enemy.y  += enemy.vy  * dt;\n132:   spawnTimer -= dt;\n133:   // ...\n134:   requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n135: }\n136: requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n137: ```\n138: \n139: ### 6.
